  800 words: public 30.33, private 7.58
You may nominate a player for trial, which stops chat for a vote. Nominating costs 1.00 from your budget this round and is paid before your messages.

It is Day 3, round 1 of at most 10. It's your turn. Your speaking budget is 2.50; you earn 2.5 per round, carrying over up to 8, and it resets each day. You may send one public message and any number of private messages (everyone sees who you messaged, but only the recipient sees the contents), or nothing. Messages are capped at 800 words. Prices grow each round after round 3, so late messages are only worth it if important. The day ends after a full round where nobody sends a message.
Message prices this round (round 1, 1.00x; next round 1.00x):
   10 words: public 1.17, private 0.29
   25 words: public 1.43, private 0.36
   50 words: public 1.90, private 0.47
  100 words: public 2.92, private 0.73
  200 words: public 5.33, private 1.33
  400 words: public 11.67, private 2.92
  800 words: public 30.33, private 7.58
You may nominate a player for trial, which stops chat for a vote. Nominating costs 1.00 from your budget this round and is paid before your messages.
